Implement a refactor in /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas. Do NOT commit, push, or run `railway up` — just edit and test.

## Background

The codebase uses asyncpg with Postgres `jsonb` columns. The current pattern is split — half the call sites pre-serialize with `json.dumps(value, default=str)` and write into `$N::jsonb` casts, the other half pass raw `dict` values. The dict-passing sites *should* fail with `asyncpg.exceptions.DataError: invalid input ... (expected str, got dict)` because `app/db.py` registers no jsonb codec on the asyncpg pool. Some have been latently broken but rarely fired in prod; one (`app/services/vision.py`) just got promoted to the hot path by commit `8ba5821 Auto-describe Discord image attachments on ingest` and is now crashing on every Discord image attachment.

## Goal

Unify on the asyncpg codec approach so every jsonb column accepts/returns Python `dict`s consistently. After this PR:
- `app/db.py` registers a `jsonb` (and `json`) codec on each pooled connection that uses `json.dumps(value, default=str)` as the encoder and `json.loads` as the decoder. The `default=str` matters — existing call sites rely on it to serialize UUIDs and datetimes.
- Every direct DB write to a jsonb column passes a Python `dict` (or `None`), never a pre-serialized `str`.
- Every read of a jsonb column returns a `dict` (this is also what the existing type annotations in `pacer.py`, `hot_context.py`, etc. assume — currently those are likely silently broken on reads, since asyncpg's default jsonb decoder returns `str`).

## Required changes

### 1. `app/db.py` — register the codec

Add an async `init` function passed to `asyncpg.create_pool`. It must register both `json` and `jsonb` from `pg_catalog`, encoder = `lambda v: json.dumps(v, default=str)`, decoder = `json.loads`.

### 2. Strip `json.dumps(..., default=str)` (or plain `json.dumps(...)`) from every site that writes to a jsonb column

Confirmed sites (you must verify each one and update — also grep for any I missed):
- `app/models/user.py:204` (pacing_preferences) — passes `json.dumps(bounded, default=str)`. Should pass `bounded` directly.
- `app/models/user.py:246-250` (signal_snapshot, preference_snapshot, llm_judgement). Strip the `json.dumps(dict(... or {}), default=str)` wrappers — pass the dicts (or `None`) directly. Watch for the `if llm_judgement is not None else None` ternary; the unwrapped form should be `dict(llm_judgement) if llm_judgement is not None else None`.
- `app/services/scheduled_job_handlers.py:270` — strip `json.dumps({"source_job_id": ...})`, pass the dict.
- `app/services/agentic.py:570` — strip `json.dumps({"triggering_message_ids": ...})`, pass the dict.
- `app/services/checkins.py:43` — strip `json.dumps(context)`, pass `context`.
- `app/services/tools/write_tools.py:161` — strip `json.dumps({context_key: ...})`, pass the dict.

You may leave the SQL `$N::jsonb` casts in place — they're harmless and document intent.

**Do NOT touch `json.dumps` calls that build payloads for the LLM API (`messages=[{"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(...)}]`)** — those are strings going to Anthropic/OpenAI, not the DB. Confirmed safe sites to leave alone: `app/services/charge.py:156`, `app/services/scoring.py:115`, `app/services/pacer.py:651`, `app/services/agentic.py:320`, `app/services/oob_check.py:218,299`, `app/services/tools/consult_perspective.py:138`. Also leave `app/services/tools/write_tools.py:94` (return value formatting), `app/routers/admin.py:46` (HTML rendering), `app/services/discord.py:350` (Discord WS opcode payload — this is a websocket frame, not DB).

### 3. Verify decoder side doesn't double-parse

Grep for `json.loads(row[...])` or `json.loads(...)` patterns where the input might be a freshly-fetched jsonb column. If any exist, drop the `json.loads` call (the codec now returns dicts directly). Be careful not to remove `json.loads` for things that are genuinely JSON strings (e.g., LLM API response bodies, env vars, files).

### 4. Update tests

The test fakes (`tests/conftest.py` `FakePool`) likely assume specific arg shapes. After your changes, run the full test suite and fix any breakage. Specifically check:
- Tests that assert on the `media_analysis`, `pacing_preferences`, `signal_snapshot`, `preference_snapshot`, `llm_judgement`, `context` (scheduled_jobs), or any other jsonb column shape.
- Tests that mock `pool.fetchrow`/`pool.execute` and check the args tuple — they may have been asserting strings, now will see dicts.

### 5. While you're in there, also re-apply two small log-noise patches

These were already authored once by me but were lost when the working tree was cleared. They're independent of the codec work but should ship in the same change:

**(a) `app/services/discord.py` `_heartbeat`** — wrap the body in `with contextlib.suppress(websockets.exceptions.ConnectionClosed):` so that benign WS reconnects don't surface as `Task exception was never retrieved` tracebacks. Current code:

```python
async def _heartbeat(self, ws: Any, interval: float) -> None:
    while not self._closed.is_set():
        await asyncio.sleep(interval)
        await ws.send(json.dumps({"op": 1, "d": None}))
```

Should become:

```python
async def _heartbeat(self, ws: Any, interval: float) -> None:
    with contextlib.suppress(websockets.exceptions.ConnectionClosed):
        while not self._closed.is_set():
            await asyncio.sleep(interval)
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"op": 1, "d": None}))
```

**(b) `app/services/charge.py` `classify_charge`** — separately catch `json.JSONDecodeError`/`ValueError`/`KeyError` from `_parse_charge` and log the offending response snippet (first 200 chars), so we can diagnose what the model is actually returning. Currently the surrounding `except Exception` swallows the parse error with a useless message:

```python
        await record_anthropic_haiku_text_response_cost(pool, _attr(response, "usage", {}))
        label, reason = _parse_charge(_response_text(response))
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("charge classification failed: %s", exc)
        return _fallback(str(exc), content)
```

Should become:

```python
        await record_anthropic_haiku_text_response_cost(pool, _attr(response, "usage", {}))
        text = _response_text(response)
        try:
            label, reason = _parse_charge(text)
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
            logger.warning(
                "charge classifier returned unparseable response (%s): %r",
                exc,
                text[:200],
            )
            return _fallback(f"unparseable response: {exc}", content)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("charge classification failed: %s", exc)
        return _fallback(str(exc), content)
```

## Workflow

1. Run `uv run pytest -q` first to capture the baseline (note any pre-existing failures so you don't blame your changes for them).
2. Make the edits.
3. Run the full test suite again. All previously-passing tests must still pass. If new failures show up, fix them — most will be `FakePool` arg-shape mismatches.
4. Do a final grep to make sure no stray `json.dumps(..., default=str)` remains on a jsonb-bound write site, and no `json.loads(row[...])` on a decoded jsonb read.
5. Report back with: list of files changed, test result (X passed), and any sites you found beyond the ones I listed.

## Constraints

- No new files unless absolutely required (tests can extend existing files).
- No CLAUDE.md / README writes.
- Don't commit, push, or invoke Railway.
- Don't refactor adjacent code — keep the diff focused on this unification + the two log-noise patches.
- If you encounter a jsonb write site that's ambiguous (e.g., the value could legitimately be a pre-serialized string from elsewhere), STOP and report rather than guessing.

Report length: under 300 words.

```
1	"""Async Postgres pool helpers."""
2	
3	from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
4	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
5	from typing import Any
6	
7	from fastapi import Request
8	
9	from app.config import get_settings
10	
11	
12	class SchemaPool:
13	    """Asyncpg pool wrapper that sets search_path on the connection used by each operation."""
14	
15	    def __init__(self, pool: Any, schema: str) -> None:
16	        self._pool = pool
17	        self._schema = schema
18	
19	    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> Any:
20	        return getattr(self._pool, name)
21	
22	    async def _prepare(self, connection: Any) -> None:
23	        if self._schema != "public":
24	            await connection.execute(f"SET LOCAL search_path TO {self._schema}, public")
25	
26	    def acquire(self) -> Any:
27	        return SchemaAcquireContext(self)
28	
29	    async def close(self) -> None:
30	        await self._pool.close()
31	
32	    async def execute(self, sql: str, *args) -> str:
33	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
34	            async with connection.transaction():
35	                await self._prepare(connection)
36	                return await connection.execute(sql, *args)
37	
38	    async def fetch(self, sql: str, *args) -> list[Any]:
39	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
40	            async with connection.transaction():
41	                await self._prepare(connection)
42	                return await connection.fetch(sql, *args)
43	
44	    async def fetchrow(self, sql: str, *args) -> Any:
45	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
46	            async with connection.transaction():
47	                await self._prepare(connection)
48	                return await connection.fetchrow(sql, *args)
49	
50	    async def fetchval(self, sql: str, *args) -> Any:
51	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
52	            async with connection.transaction():
53	                await self._prepare(connection)
54	                return await connection.fetchval(sql, *args)
55	
56	
57	class SchemaAcquireContext:
58	    def __init__(self, schema_pool: SchemaPool) -> None:
59	        self._schema_pool = schema_pool
60	        self._inner = None
61	        self._connection = None
62	
63	    async def __aenter__(self) -> Any:
64	        self._inner = self._schema_pool._pool.acquire()
65	        self._connection = await self._inner.__aenter__()
66	        return self._connection
67	
68	    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb) -> bool:
69	        return await self._inner.__aexit__(exc_type, exc, tb)
70	
71	
72	@asynccontextmanager
73	async def db_lifespan(app: Any) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
74	    """Create an asyncpg pool for the app lifetime."""
75	    import asyncpg
76	
77	    settings = get_settings()
78	    raw_pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(settings.database_url, statement_cache_size=0)
79	    pool = SchemaPool(raw_pool, settings.database_schema)
80	    app.state.pool = pool
81	    try:
82	        yield
83	    finally:
84	        await pool.close()
85	
86	
87	def get_pool(request: Request) -> Any:
88	    """FastAPI dependency returning the application database pool."""
89	    return request.app.state.pool
90	
91	
92	async def ping(pool: Any) -> None:
93	    """Raise if the database is not reachable."""
94	    async with pool.acquire() as connection:
95	        await connection.execute("SELECT 1")
96
```

```
22	def _clamp_float(value: Any, default: float, minimum: float, maximum: float) -> float:
23	    try:
24	        number = float(value)
25	    except (TypeError, ValueError):

27	    return min(max(number, minimum), maximum)

35	    return min(max(number, minimum), maximum)

38	def _coerce_bool(value: Any, default: bool) -> bool:
39	    if isinstance(value, bool):
40	        return value
41	    if isinstance(value, str):
42	        lowered = value.strip().lower()
43	        if lowered in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}:
44	            return True
45	        if lowered in {"0", "false", "no", "off"}:
46	            return False
47	    return default
```

```
11	EvalStatus = Literal["pass", "fail", "skipped"]
12	
13	
14	@dataclass(frozen=True)
15	class EvalRun:
16	    id: UUID
17	    run_at: datetime
18	    prompt_version: str
19	    scenarios_passed: int
20	    scenarios_failed: int

36	
37	async def create_eval_run(
38	    pool: Any,
39	    *,
40	    prompt_version: str,
41	    scenarios_passed: int = 0,
42	    scenarios_failed: int = 0,
43	    total_cost_usd: Decimal | float | str = Decimal("0"),
44	    git_sha: str | None = None,
45	    notes: str | None = None,
46	) -> UUID:
47	    row = await pool.fetchrow(
48	        """
49	        INSERT INTO public.eval_runs (
50	            prompt_version, scenarios_passed, scenarios_failed, total_cost_usd, git_sha, notes
51	        )
52	        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
53	        RETURNING id
54	        """,
55	        prompt_version,
56	        scenarios_passed,
57	        scenarios_failed,
58	        Decimal(str(total_cost_usd)),
59	        git_sha,
60	        notes,
61	    )
62	    return row["id"]
63	
64	
65	async def update_eval_run_summary(
66	    pool: Any,
67	    run_id: UUID,
68	    *,
69	    scenarios_passed: int,
70	    scenarios_failed: int,
71	    total_cost_usd: Decimal | float | str,
72	    notes: str | None = None,
73	) -> None:
74	    await pool.execute(
75	        """
76	        UPDATE public.eval_runs
77	        SET scenarios_passed = $1,
78	            scenarios_failed = $2,
79	            total_cost_usd = $3,
80	            notes = COALESCE($4, notes)
81	        WHERE id = $5
82	        """,
83	        scenarios_passed,
84	        scenarios_failed,
85	        Decimal(str(total_cost_usd)),
86	        notes,
87	        run_id,
88	    )
89	
90	
91	async def record_eval_result(
92	    pool: Any,
93	    *,
94	    run_id: UUID,
95	    scenario_name: str,
96	    status: EvalStatus,
97	    judge_verdicts: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
98	    tool_calls: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
99	    failure_reason: str | None = None,
100	) -> UUID:
101	    if status not in {"pass", "fail", "skipped"}:
102	        raise ValueError("status must be one of: pass, fail, skipped")
103	    row = await pool.fetchrow(
104	        """
105	        INSERT INTO public.eval_results (
106	            run_id, scenario_name, status, judge_verdicts, tool_calls, failure_reason
107	        )
108	        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4::jsonb, $5::jsonb, $6)
109	        RETURNING id
110	        """,
111	        run_id,
112	        scenario_name,
113	        status,
114	        json.dumps(judge_verdicts or []),
115	        json.dumps(tool_calls or []),
116	        failure_reason,
117	    )
118	    return row["id"]
119	
120	
121	async def list_eval_runs(pool: Any, *, limit: int = 25) -> list[EvalRun]:
122	    rows = await pool.fetch(
123	        """
124	        SELECT id, run_at, prompt_version, scenarios_passed, scenarios_failed,
125	               total_cost_usd, git_sha, notes
126	        FROM public.eval_runs
127	        ORDER BY run_at DESC
128	        LIMIT $1
129	        """,
130	        limit,
131	    )
132	    return [_eval_run_from_row(row) for row in rows]
133	
134	
135	async def list_eval_results(pool: Any, run_id: UUID) -> list[EvalResult]:
136	    rows = await pool.fetch(
137	        """
138	        SELECT id, run_id, scenario_name, status, judge_verdicts, tool_calls, failure_reason
139	        FROM public.eval_results
140	        WHERE run_id = $1
141	        ORDER BY scenario_name ASC
142	        """,
143	        run_id,
144	    )
145	    return [_eval_result_from_row(row) for row in rows]
146	
147	
148	def _eval_run_from_row(row: Any) -> EvalRun:
149	    return EvalRun(
150	        id=row["id"],
151	        run_at=row["run_at"],
152	        prompt_version=row["prompt_version"],
153	        scenarios_passed=row["scenarios_passed"],
154	        scenarios_failed=row["scenarios_failed"],
155	        total_cost_usd=Decimal(str(row["total_cost_usd"])),
156	        git_sha=row.get("git_sha") if hasattr(row, "get") else row["git_sha"],
157	        notes=row.get("notes") if hasattr(row, "get") else row["notes"],
158	    )
159	
160	
161	def _eval_result_from_row(row: Any) -> EvalResult:
162	    return EvalResult(
163	        id=row["id"],
164	        run_id=row["run_id"],
165	        scenario_name=row["scenario_name"],
166	        status=row["status"],
167	        judge_verdicts=_jsonb_list(row["judge_verdicts"]),
168	        tool_calls=_jsonb_list(row["tool_calls"]),
169	        failure_reason=row.get("failure_reason") if hasattr(row, "get") else row["failure_reason"],
170	    )
171	
172	
173	def _jsonb_list(value: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
174	    if value is None:
175	        return []
176	    if isinstance(value, str):
177	        loaded = json.loads(value)
178	    else:
179	        loaded = value
180	    if not isinstance(loaded, list):
181	        raise ValueError("jsonb value must decode to a list")
182	    return loaded
183
```

> AGENT

The `tool_calls` table inserts pre-serialized strings (`args.model_dump_json()` and `_json_payload(result)` which calls `json.dumps`). These are JSONB writes. With the codec, encoder=`json.dumps(value, default=str)` will be applied. If we pass a string like `'{"foo":"bar"}'`, the codec will JSON-encode it as the string literal `'"{\\"foo\\":\\"bar\\"}"'`, which is wrong. So we need to pass dicts.

Let me fix `_log_tool_call`:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/app/services/tools/write_tools.py",
  "old_string": "def _json_payload(value: BaseModel | dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):\n        return value.model_dump_json()\n    return json.dumps(value, default=str)\n\n\nasync def _log_tool_call(\n    ctx: TurnContext,\n    name: str,\n    args: BaseModel,\n    started_at: datetime,\n    result: BaseModel | dict[str, Any],\n) -> None:\n    duration_ms = max(0, int((datetime.now(UTC) - started_at).total_seconds() * 1000))\n    await ctx.pool.execute(\n        \"\"\"\n        INSERT INTO tool_calls (turn_id, tool_name, arguments, result, called_at, duration_ms)\n        VALUES ($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4::jsonb, $5, $6)\n        \"\"\",\n        ctx.turn_id,\n        name,\n        args.model_dump_json(),\n        _json_payload(result),\n        started_at,\n        duration_ms,\n    )",
  "new_string": "def _json_payload(value: BaseModel | dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):\n        return value.model_dump_json()\n    return json.dumps(value, default=str)\n\n\ndef _jsonb_payload(value: BaseModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):\n        return json.loads(value.model_dump_json())\n    return value\n\n\nasync def _log_tool_call(\n    ctx: TurnContext,\n    name: str,\n    args: BaseModel,\n    started_at: datetime,\n    result: BaseModel | dict[str, Any],\n) -> None:\n    duration_ms = max(0, int((datetime.now(UTC) - started_at).total_seconds() * 1000))\n    await ctx.pool.execute(\n        \"\"\"\n        INSERT INTO tool_calls (turn_id, tool_name, arguments, result, called_at, duration_ms)\n        VALUES ($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4::jsonb, $5, $6)\n        \"\"\",\n        ctx.turn_id,\n        name,\n        _jsonb_payload(args),\n        _jsonb_payload(result),\n        started_at,\n        duration_ms,\n    )"
}
```

## Report

### Files changed

- `app/db.py` — registered jsonb/json codec on every pooled connection (encoder = `json.dumps(v, default=str)`, decoder = `json.loads`).
- `app/models/user.py` — `update_user_pacing_preferences` and `record_pacing_event` now pass dicts (or `None`) directly.
- `app/services/scheduled_job_handlers.py` — weekly_summary insert passes dict.
- `app/services/agentic.py` — deferred_turn insert passes dict.
- `app/services/checkins.py` — checkin insert passes dict.
- `app/services/tools/write_tools.py` — `_schedule_context_job` passes dict; new `_jsonb_payload` helper feeds `_log_tool_call`'s arguments/result as dicts (was sending pre-serialized strings into jsonb columns, would have double-encoded under the new codec).
- `app/services/discord.py` — wrapped `_heartbeat` body in `contextlib.suppress(websockets.exceptions.ConnectionClosed)`.
- `app/services/charge.py` — `classify_charge` now catches `_parse_charge` errors separately and logs the offending response snippet.
- `evals/results.py` — `record_eval_result` passes `judge_verdicts`/`tool_calls` as lists.
- `evals/factories.py` — `_seed_scheduled_jobs` passes context dict.
- `tests/conftest.py` — added `_coerce_jsonb` helper and replaced every `json.loads(...)` on jsonb-write args (pacing prefs, scheduled job context, pacing events, eval_results, tool_calls) with it so the FakePool tolerates the new dict shape.

### Sites found beyond the listed ones

- `evals/results.py:114-115` (`record_eval_result`)
- `evals/factories.py:313` (`_seed_scheduled_jobs`)
- `app/services/tools/write_tools.py:_log_tool_call` — was passing JSON strings into jsonb columns. Latently broken on real Postgres; would have started double-encoding with the new codec, so had to be unified.

### Tests

`uv run pytest -q` — 299 passed, 3 skipped, 2 failed. Both failures (`test_hot_context.py::test_build_hot_context_returns_expected_fields` and `::test_render_hot_context_respects_default_token_budget`) are pre-existing on baseline (date-dependent, unrelated to this change).

### Read sites verified

The `json.loads` calls remaining in production code parse LLM response text or non-DB inputs (Anthropic responses in `pacer/charge/scoring/oob_check/consult_perspective`, the WhatsApp webhook body in `routers/whatsapp.py`, Discord WS frames in `services/discord.py`, the defensive `_normalize_json_object` in `models/user.py`, and the defensive `_as_context` in `scheduled_job_handlers.py`). None decode a fresh jsonb column.

Cleanup task in /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas. Do NOT commit, push, or run `railway up` — just edit and test.

## Context

A previous change registered an asyncpg `jsonb`/`json` codec in `app/db.py` (encoder = `json.dumps(v, default=str)`, decoder = `json.loads`) on every pooled connection. After that change, every read of a jsonb column returns a `dict`/`list`/scalar (already deserialized), and every write accepts a `dict`/`list`/scalar directly.

Before that change, the codebase had defensive coercion helpers because asyncpg's default jsonb decoder returns `str`. Those helpers are now dead weight: the value they receive is already the right type.

## What to remove

Concretely:
- `app/models/user.py` defines `_normalize_json_object` and uses it on freshly-fetched jsonb values to coerce `str → dict`. Since the codec now guarantees a dict, the helper is redundant at every read-side call site, and the function itself can likely be deleted.
- `app/services/scheduled_job_handlers.py` has a similar defensive helper `_as_context` (per the prior agent's report). Same logic applies — if it's coercing a freshly-fetched jsonb value, the coercion is now a no-op.
- Search for any other defensive `json.loads(...)`-on-jsonb-row or `isinstance(..., str)`-then-parse patterns elsewhere (`hot_context.py`, `pacer.py`, `inbound.py`, anywhere reading jsonb columns).

## Constraints

- Only remove coercion that is provably dead given the codec. Some helpers may still be needed for inputs that come from non-DB sources (LLM responses, webhook bodies, env vars). Leave those alone.
- Before removing each helper, grep for ALL its call sites. If a call site receives data from somewhere other than a freshly-fetched jsonb column, do not remove the call there — and the helper must stay.
- If a helper is still used in one place but not others, drop the dead call sites and keep the helper. Do not introduce new abstractions.
- Don't touch the codec itself or any of the recent jsonb write-site changes.
- No new files, no CLAUDE.md / README writes, no scope creep.

## Workflow

1. Read `app/db.py` to confirm the codec setup.
2. Find every defensive jsonb coercion helper and every direct `json.loads`/`isinstance(str)` defense around DB reads. For each, trace its call sites.
3. Decide per-helper: fully remove, partially remove call sites, or leave alone. Briefly justify each decision in your report.
4. Make the edits.
5. Run `uv run pytest -q`. Baseline before your changes was 301 passed, 3 skipped, 0 failed. Match or improve. Fix any breakage your changes cause.
6. Report back with: files changed, helpers removed/kept (with reason), test result, and any borderline cases you left in place for safety.

Report length: under 250 words.

```
1	"""Async Postgres pool helpers."""
2	
3	import json
4	from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
5	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
6	from typing import Any
7	
8	from fastapi import Request

12	
13	def _jsonb_encoder(value: Any) -> str:
14	    return json.dumps(value, default=str)
15	
16	
17	async def _init_connection(connection: Any) -> None:
18	    """Register Python dict <-> Postgres jsonb/json codec on each pooled connection."""
19	    for type_name in ("jsonb", "json"):
20	        await connection.set_type_codec(
21	            type_name,
22	            schema="pg_catalog",
23	            encoder=_jsonb_encoder,
24	            decoder=json.loads,
25	            format="text",
26	        )
27	
28	
29	class SchemaPool:
30	    """Asyncpg pool wrapper that sets search_path on the connection used by each operation."""
31	
32	    def __init__(self, pool: Any, schema: str) -> None:
33	        self._pool = pool
34	        self._schema = schema
35	
36	    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> Any:
37	        return getattr(self._pool, name)
38	
39	    async def _prepare(self, connection: Any) -> None:
40	        if self._schema != "public":
41	            await connection.execute(f"SET LOCAL search_path TO {self._schema}, public")
42	
43	    def acquire(self) -> Any:
44	        return SchemaAcquireContext(self)
45	
46	    async def close(self) -> None:
47	        await self._pool.close()
48	
49	    async def execute(self, sql: str, *args) -> str:
50	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
51	            async with connection.transaction():
52	                await self._prepare(connection)
53	                return await connection.execute(sql, *args)
54	
55	    async def fetch(self, sql: str, *args) -> list[Any]:
56	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
57	            async with connection.transaction():
58	                await self._prepare(connection)
59	                return await connection.fetch(sql, *args)
60	
61	    async def fetchrow(self, sql: str, *args) -> Any:
62	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
63	            async with connection.transaction():
64	                await self._prepare(connection)
65	                return await connection.fetchrow(sql, *args)
66	
67	    async def fetchval(self, sql: str, *args) -> Any:
68	        async with self._pool.acquire() as connection:
69	            async with connection.transaction():
70	                await self._prepare(connection)
71	                return await connection.fetchval(sql, *args)
72	
73	
74	class SchemaAcquireContext:
75	    def __init__(self, schema_pool: SchemaPool) -> None:
76	        self._schema_pool = schema_pool
77	        self._inner = None
78	        self._connection = None
79	
80	    async def __aenter__(self) -> Any:
81	        self._inner = self._schema_pool._pool.acquire()
82	        self._connection = await self._inner.__aenter__()
83	        return self._connection
84	
85	    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb) -> bool:
86	        return await self._inner.__aexit__(exc_type, exc, tb)
87	
88	
89	@asynccontextmanager
90	async def db_lifespan(app: Any) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
91	    """Create an asyncpg pool for the app lifetime."""
92	    import asyncpg
93	
94	    settings = get_settings()
95	    raw_pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(
96	        settings.database_url,
97	        statement_cache_size=0,
98	        init=_init_connection,
99	    )
100	    pool = SchemaPool(raw_pool, settings.database_schema)
101	    app.state.pool = pool
102	    try:
103	        yield
104	    finally:
105	        await pool.close()
106	
107	
108	def get_pool(request: Request) -> Any:
109	    """FastAPI dependency returning the application database pool."""
110	    return request.app.state.pool
111	
112	
113	async def ping(pool: Any) -> None:
114	    """Raise if the database is not reachable."""
115	    async with pool.acquire() as connection:
116	        await connection.execute("SELECT 1")
```

```
22	def _clamp_float(value: Any, default: float, minimum: float, maximum: float) -> float:
23	    try:
24	        number = float(value)
25	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
26	        return default
27	    return min(max(number, minimum), maximum)
28	
29	
30	def _clamp_int(value: Any, default: int, minimum: int, maximum: int) -> int:
31	    try:
32	        number = int(value)
33	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
34	        return default
35	    return min(max(number, minimum), maximum)
36

38	def _coerce_bool(value: Any, default: bool) -> bool:
39	    if isinstance(value, bool):
40	        return value
41	    if isinstance(value, str):
42	        lowered = value.strip().lower()
43	        if lowered in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}:
44	            return True
45	        if lowered in {"0", "false", "no", "off"}:
46	            return False
47	    return default

50	def resolve_pacing_preferences(
51	    raw_preferences: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
52	    settings: Settings | None = None,
53	) -> dict[str, Any]:
54	    """Return a bounded pacing preference snapshot safe for timing decisions."""
55	    settings = settings or get_settings()
56	    raw = dict(raw_preferences or {})
57	    max_wait_s = _clamp_float(
58	        raw.get("max_wait_s"),
59	        settings.discord_pacing_max_wait_s,
60	        max(settings.discord_pacing_min_wait_s, 1.0),
61	        60.0,
62	    )
63	    resolved = {
64	        "enabled": _coerce_bool(raw.get("enabled"), settings.discord_pacing_enabled),
65	        "burst_window_s": _clamp_float(
66	            raw.get("burst_window_s"),
67	            settings.discord_pacing_burst_window_s,
68	            0.25,
69	            min(max_wait_s, 15.0),
70	        ),
71	        "min_wait_s": _clamp_float(
72	            raw.get("min_wait_s"),
73	            settings.discord_pacing_min_wait_s,
74	            0.0,
75	            min(settings.discord_pacing_max_wait_s, 10.0),
76	        ),
77	        "max_wait_s": max_wait_s,
78	        "typing_grace_s": _clamp_float(

81	            0.5,

83	        ),
84	        "max_typing_wait_s": _clamp_float(

87	            1.0,
88	            90.0,
89	        ),

121	    if resolved["answer_typing_min_s"] > resolved["answer_typing_max_s"]:
122	        resolved["answer_typing_min_s"] = resolved["answer_typing_max_s"]

126	def _normalize_json_object(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
```

```
24	def _utc_now() -> datetime:
25	    return datetime.now(UTC)
26

28	def _as_context(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
29	    if value is None:
30	        return {}
31	    if isinstance(value, dict):
32	        return dict(value)
33	    if isinstance(value, str):
34	        return json.loads(value)
35	    return dict(value)

38	def _zoneinfo(name: str) -> ZoneInfo:
39	    try:
40	        return ZoneInfo(name)
41	    except ZoneInfoNotFoundError:
42	        logger.warning("unknown user timezone %s; falling back to UTC", name)
43	        return ZoneInfo("UTC")
44	
45	
46	def _time_value(value: Any) -> time:
47	    if isinstance(value, time):
48	        return value
49	    if isinstance(value, str):
50	        hour, minute, *_ = value.split(":")
51	        return time(int(hour), int(minute))
52	    return time(9, 0)
53

55	def next_weekly_summary_at(user_row: dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime | None = None) -> datetime:
56	    now = now or _utc_now()
57	    if now.tzinfo is None:
58	        now = now.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
59	    zone = _zoneinfo(user_row.get("timezone") or "UTC")
60	    local_now = now.astimezone(zone)
61	    target_day = int(user_row.get("weekly_summary_day", 1))
62	    current_day = (local_now.weekday() + 1) % 7
63	    days_ahead = (target_day - current_day) % 7
64	    target_time = _time_value(user_row.get("weekly_summary_time", "09:00"))
65	    candidate_date = local_now.date() + timedelta(days=days_ahead)
66	    candidate = datetime.combine(candidate_date, target_time, zone)
67	    if candidate <= local_now:
68	        candidate += timedelta(days=7)
69	    return candidate.astimezone(UTC)
70

72	class ScheduledJobHandlers:
73	    def __init__(self, pool: Any, *, settings: Settings | None = None) -> None:
74	        self.pool = pool
75	        self.settings = settings or get_settings()
76	
77	    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
78	        return {
79	            "weekly_summary": self.handle_weekly_summary,
80	            "checkin": self.handle_checkin,
81	            "watch_item_due": self.handle_watch_item_due,
82	            "oob_review": self.handle_oob_review,
83	            "heartbeat": self.handle_heartbeat,
84	            "deferred_turn": self.handle_deferred_turn,
85	        }
86	
87	    async def handle_weekly_summary(self, job: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
88	        user_row = await self._fetch_user_schedule(job["user_id"])
89	        user = _user_from_row(user_row)
90	        summary = await self._weekly_summary_counts(user.id)
91	        content = (
92	            f"Hi {user.name}, this week we had {summary['conversation_count']} conversations "
93	            f"and touched on {summary['ongoing_count']} ongoing things. Want to talk through anything? Just ask."
94	        )
95	        await send_outbound(
96	            self.pool,
97	            user,
98	            content,
99	            template_fallback=TemplateCall(
100	                "weekly_summary",
101	                [user.name, str(summary["conversation_count"]), str(summary["ongoing_count"])],
102	            ),
103	        )
104	        await run_decay_housekeeping(self.pool)
105	        await schedule_next_weekly_summary(self.pool, user_row, now=_utc_now(), source_job_id=job["id"])
106	
107	    async def handle_checkin(self, job: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
108	        user = await fetch_user_by_id(self.pool, job["user_id"])
109	        context = _as_context(job.get("context"))
110	        metadata = {"kind": "checkin", "context": {**context, "delayed": bool(job.get("delayed"))}}
111	        if await _can_send_freeform(self.pool, user, self.settings):
112	            await run_agentic_job(user, metadata)
113	            return
114	        await send_outbound(
115	            self.pool,
116	            user,
117	            f"Hi {user.name}, been a bit -- anything on your mind? Just message me back when you're ready.",
118	            template_fallback=TemplateCall("checkin_nudge", [user.name]),
119	        )
120	
121	    async def handle_watch_item_due(self, job: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
122	        user = await fetch_user_by_id(self.pool, job["user_id"])
123	        context = _as_context(job.get("context"))
124	        watch_item = await self._fetch_watch_item(context.get("watch_item_id"))
125	        metadata = {
126	            "kind": "watch_item_due",
127	            "context": {
128	                **context,
129	                "watch_item": watch_item,
130	                "delayed": bool(job.get("delayed")),
131	            },
132	        }
133	        if await _can_send_freeform(self.pool, user, self.settings):
134	            await run_agentic_job(user, metadata)
135	            return
136	        await schedule_checkin_job(
137	            self.pool,

140	            context={"kind": "watch_item_due", **metadata["context"]},
141	        )
142	
143	    async def handle_oob_review(self, job: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
144	        user = await fetch_user_by_id(self.pool, job["user_id"])
145	        context = _as_context(job.get("context"))
146	        await run_agentic_job(
147	            user,
148	            {
149	                "kind": "oob_review",
150	                "context": {**context, "delayed": bool(job.get("delayed"))},
151	            },
152	        )

154	    async def handle_heartbeat(self, job: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
155	        logger.info("scheduled heartbeat fired job_id=%s scheduled_for=%s", job["id"], job["scheduled_for"])
156	        await purge_expired_deletions(self.pool)
157	
158	    async def handle_deferred_turn(self, job: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
159	        user = await fetch_user_by_id(self.pool, job["user_id"])
160	        context = _as_context(job.get("context"))
161	        message_ids = [UUID(value) for value in context.get("triggering_message_ids", [])]
162	        if message_ids:
163	            await self.pool.execute(
164	                "UPDATE messages SET processing_state='raw' WHERE id = ANY($1)",
165	                message_ids,
166	            )
167	            await run_agentic_turn(message_ids, user)
168	
169	    async def _fetch_user_schedule(self, user_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any]:
170	        row = await self.pool.fetchrow(
171	            """
172	            SELECT id, name, phone, timezone, weekly_summary_enabled,
173	                   weekly_summary_day, weekly_summary_time
174	            FROM users
175	            WHERE id = $1
176	            """,
177	            user_id,
178	        )
179	        if row is None:
180	            raise ValueError(f"user not found for scheduled job: {user_id}")
181	        return dict(row)
182	
183	    async def _weekly_summary_counts(self, user_id: UUID) -> dict[str, int]:
184	        row = await self.pool.fetchrow(
185	            """
186	            SELECT
187	                (
188	                    SELECT COUNT(*)
189	                    FROM messages
190	                    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
191	                      AND sent_at >= now() - interval '7 days'
192	                      AND (sender_id = $1 OR recipient_id = $1)
193	                )::int AS conversation_count,
194	                (
195	                    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM themes WHERE status = 'active')
196	                    +
197	                    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM watch_items WHERE owner_user_id = $1 AND status = 'open')
198	                )::int AS ongoing_count
199	            """,
200	            user_id,
201	        )
202	        return {
203	            "conversation_count": int(row["conversation_count"] or 0),
204	            "ongoing_count": int(row["ongoing_count"] or 0),
205	        }
206	
207	    async def _fetch_watch_item(self, watch_item_id: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
208	        if watch_item_id is None:
209	            return None
210	        row = await self.pool.fetchrow(
211	            """
212	            SELECT id, owner_user_id, content, due_at, status
213	            FROM watch_items
214	            WHERE id = $1
215	            """,
216	            watch_item_id,
217	        )
218	        return dict(row) if row is not None else None
219

221	def _user_from_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> User:
222	    return User(id=row["id"], name=row["name"], phone=row["phone"], timezone=row["timezone"])
223	
224	
225	async def _can_send_freeform(pool: Any, user: User, settings: Settings | None = None) -> bool:
226	    settings = settings or get_settings()
227	    if settings.messaging_provider.strip().lower() == "discord":
228	        return True
229	    return await _within_whatsapp_window(pool, user)
230	
231	
232	async def _within_whatsapp_window(pool: Any, user: User) -> bool:
233	    last_inbound_at = await pool.fetchval(
234	        "SELECT MAX(sent_at) FROM messages WHERE sender_id=$1 AND direction='inbound'",
235	        user.id,
236	    )
237	    if last_inbound_at is None:
238	        return False
239	    if last_inbound_at.tzinfo is None:
240	        last_inbound_at = last_inbound_at.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
241	    return _utc_now() - last_inbound_at < timedelta(hours=24)
242	
243	
244	async def schedule_next_weekly_summary(
245	    pool: Any,
246	    user_row: dict[str, Any],
247	    *,
248	    now: datetime | None = None,
249	    source_job_id: Any | None = None,
250	) -> Any | None:
251	    if not user_row.get("weekly_summary_enabled", True):
252	        return None
253	    scheduled_for = next_weekly_summary_at(user_row, now=now)
254	    return await pool.fetchrow(
255	        """
256	        INSERT INTO scheduled_jobs (user_id, job_type, scheduled_for, context, status)
257	        SELECT $1, 'weekly_summary', $2, $3::jsonb, 'pending'
258	        WHERE NOT EXISTS (
259	            SELECT 1
260	            FROM scheduled_jobs
261	            WHERE user_id = $1
262	              AND job_type = 'weekly_summary'
263	              AND status = 'pending'
264	              AND ($4::uuid IS NULL OR id <> $4::uuid)
265	        )
266	        RETURNING id, scheduled_for
267	        """,
268	        user_row["id"],
269	        scheduled_for,
270	        {"source_job_id": str(source_job_id) if source_job_id is not None else None},
271	        source_job_id,
272	    )
273	
274	
275	async def seed_weekly_summaries(pool: Any, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> list[Any]:
276	    rows = await pool.fetch(
277	        """
278	        SELECT id, name, phone, timezone, weekly_summary_enabled,
279	               weekly_summary_day, weekly_summary_time
280	        FROM users
281	        WHERE weekly_summary_enabled = true
282	        """
283	    )
284	    inserted = []
285	    for row in rows:
286	        inserted.append(await schedule_next_weekly_summary(pool, dict(row), now=now))
287	    return inserted
288	
289	
290	async def schedule_checkin_job(
291	    pool: Any,
292	    user_id: UUID,
293	    *,
294	    scheduled_for: datetime,
295	    context: dict[str, Any],
296	) -> Any:
297	    _old, row = await schedule_checkin_record(
298	        pool,
299	        user_id,
300	        scheduled_for=scheduled_for,
301	        context=context,
302	    )
303	    return row
```

```
90	def _compact_json_value(value: Any, *, text_limit: int = 180) -> Any:
91	    if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, int, float)):
92	        return value
93	    if isinstance(value, UUID):
94	        return str(value)
95	    if isinstance(value, str):
96	        return value if len(value) <= text_limit else value[: text_limit - 3] + "..."
97	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
98	        compact: dict[str, Any] = {}
99	        for key, item in value.items():
100	            if item is None:
101	                continue
102	            compact[str(key)] = _compact_json_value(item, text_limit=text_limit)
103	        return compact
104	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
105	        return [_compact_json_value(item, text_limit=text_limit) for item in list(value)[:8]]
106	    return str(value)

214	async def _record_response_cost(pool: Any, usage: Any) -> None:
215	    settings = get_settings()
216	    input_price = settings.anthropic_input_usd_per_mtok
217	    output_price = settings.anthropic_output_usd_per_mtok
218	    input_tokens = _usage_tokens(usage, "input_tokens")
219	    cache_create = _usage_tokens(usage, "cache_creation_input_tokens")
220	    cache_read = _usage_tokens(usage, "cache_read_input_tokens")
221	    output_tokens = _usage_tokens(usage, "output_tokens")
222	    regular_input_tokens = max(0, input_tokens - cache_create - cache_read)
223	    dollars = (
224	        regular_input_tokens * input_price
225	        + cache_create * input_price * 1.25
226	        + cache_read * input_price * 0.10
227	        + output_tokens * output_price
228	    ) / 1_000_000
229	    await record_llm_cost(pool, "text", dollars)
230

232	async def _create_message_with_retry(
233	    client: Any,
234	    *,
235	    ctx: TurnContext,
236	    system: list[dict[str, Any]],
237	    tools: list[dict[str, Any]],
238	    messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
239	    model: str | None = None,
240	    max_tokens: int = 1200,
241	) -> Any:
242	    settings = get_settings()
243	    last_error: Exception | None = None
244	    for attempt in range(2):
245	        if not await is_under_cap(ctx.pool, "text"):
246	            raise SpendCapExceeded("text LLM spend cap exceeded")
247	        try:
248	            response = await client.messages.create(
249	                model=model or settings.conversational_model,
250	                max_tokens=max_tokens,
251	                system=system,
252	                messages=messages,
253	                tools=tools,
254	            )
255	        except Exception as exc:  # Anthropic SDK transient subclasses vary by version.
256	            last_error = exc
257	            if attempt == 0:
258	                logger.warning("anthropic message create failed; retrying once: %s", exc)
259	                continue
260	            raise LLMPhaseError(str(exc)) from exc
261	        await _record_response_cost(ctx.pool, _attr(response, "usage", {}))
262	        return response
263	    raise LLMPhaseError(str(last_error or "anthropic message create failed"))
```

> DEVELOPER

Two related fixes in /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas. Do NOT commit, push, or run `railway up` — just edit and test.

## Background

The Discord bot (Véas) just had auto-describe-on-ingest enabled for image attachments. Two visible problems remain:

1. **Vision summarizes, doesn't transcribe.** The prompt at `app/services/vision.py:16-23` (`MEDIA_EXPLAIN_PROMPT`) asks for a "compact, queryable note" with screenshots/text *mentioned*. Result: WhatsApp screenshots get described as "a conversation between you and [REDACTED]" rather than having the verbatim lines transcribed. The user wants any visible text returned verbatim alongside the description.

2. **Split-reply race.** When a Discord message contains both text content and an image attachment, the bot replies twice: first turn says "no image came through" (text ingested + agentic turn fired before vision finished), then a second turn fires after `vision.handle_image` writes `media_analysis` and `coalescer.add(..., source="media")` enqueues a follow-up. The user sees two contradictory replies. They want a single reply that already knows about the image.

## Task 1 — Update the vision prompt

File: `app/services/vision.py`, constant `MEDIA_EXPLAIN_PROMPT`.

Rewrite the prompt so the model returns BOTH a compact description AND a verbatim transcription of any text visible in the image (UI labels, screenshot message bodies, signs, handwriting if legible, etc.). Keep it plain text (the existing pipeline expects plain text — see `_extract_output_text` and `_analysis_payload`). Suggested shape: a description paragraph followed by a `Visible text:` section that captures every readable string in reading order, with empty/none-detected handled gracefully ("Visible text: none" or omitted).

Constraints on the prompt:
- Do not change the return format (still plain text written into `explanation`/`description`).
- Keep the existing safety rails: no identifying unknown people, no inferring protected traits, no advice.
- Note uncertainty when text is partial or unreadable (don't hallucinate text that isn't there).

Bump `settings.vision_detail` consideration: if it defaults to `"low"`, OCR will be lossy. Check `app/config.py` and the relevant default. If it's `"low"`, change the default to `"high"` so verbatim transcription actually works — but only if the existing setting is a sensible knob to flip globally. If it's already `"high"` or `"auto"`, leave it.

## Task 2 — Eliminate the split reply

The race lives in the Discord ingest path. Trace it:
- `app/services/discord.py:_handle_message` (around line 372) calls `process_inbound(self.pool, message_to_meta_payload(message), self.coalescer)`.
- `message_to_meta_payload` (around line 216) returns a payload with potentially multiple message items: text item(s) and image item(s).
- `app/services/inbound.py:process_inbound` iterates the items. The text item triggers a coalescer entry quickly. The image item invokes `vision.handle_image` which downloads + calls OpenAI (slow), then calls `coalescer.add(..., source="media")` after `media_analysis` is written.

Goal: when a single Discord message contains both text and image attachments, the agentic turn must NOT fire until the image's `media_analysis` is populated, so the LLM has the description in its hot context on the first (and only) reply.

Investigate the coalescer / inbound logic and pick the cleanest intervention point. Two reasonable shapes:

- **Option A (preferred if straightforward):** in `process_inbound`, when the inbound batch contains both text and image items, await `vision.handle_image` before enqueuing the text-driven coalescer entry — so by the time the agentic turn fires, `media_analysis` is set. Effectively merge the two turns at the source.
- **Option B:** add a small "hold" / debounce in the coalescer — if an image attachment from the same message ID is currently being processed, a text-driven add should wait (with a sensible timeout, e.g. `media_fetch_timeout_s + a few seconds`) before firing the agentic turn.
- **Option C:** in `process_inbound`, suppress the text-driven coalescer add when an image is also present in the same payload, relying on the `source="media"` follow-up to be the single turn that includes both. Make sure that follow-up turn actually has the text content available.

Pick the option that's least invasive and avoids holding asyncpg connections / pool slots while waiting on OpenAI. Document your choice briefly.

Edge cases to handle:
- Multiple images in one message: wait for all of them.
- Vision fails (caught in `vision.handle_image`'s `except Exception`): the agentic turn must still fire, with whatever fallback `media_analysis` was written (`{"error": "vision_failed"}`). Don't deadlock the user's reply on a vision failure.
- Vision daily-cap exceeded path: same — must not block the reply forever.
- Pure-text Discord messages (no image): no behavior change.
- Pure-image Discord messages (no text): no behavior change — the existing `source="media"` enqueue stays.
- WhatsApp ingest: this same `process_inbound` is shared with WhatsApp. Same race likely exists there. If your fix in `process_inbound` is platform-agnostic, it'll fix both. If it has to be Discord-specific, scope it to Discord and note WhatsApp as a follow-up.

## Tests

Add tests for both fixes:
- A vision prompt test isn't very meaningful (the prompt is a string going to OpenAI), but if there's an existing test that asserts the prompt mentions key constraints, update its expected substrings.
- For the race fix: add a test under `tests/test_discord.py` (or `tests/test_inbound.py` if more appropriate) that simulates a Discord message with both text and image attachments, fakes vision (success path + failure path), and asserts the agentic turn fires exactly once, with `media_analysis` populated when it fires.

Run `uv run pytest -q` and report. Pre-existing baseline is 298 passed, 3 skipped, 2 failed (`test_hot_context.py::test_render_hot_context_respects_default_token_budget` and `::test_render_hot_context_truncates_without_dropping_oob`) — those are unrelated and can stay failing. Anything else failing is on your changes.

## Constraints

- No new files unless absolutely required.
- No CLAUDE.md / README writes.
- Don't commit, push, or invoke Railway.
- Don't refactor adjacent code beyond what's needed for these two fixes.
- If something looks ambiguous (e.g., the coalescer behavior or how `source="media"` interacts with hot context), STOP and report rather than guessing.

Report back with: files changed, which option you chose for the race (A/B/C/other) and one-sentence why, the new prompt verbatim, any vision_detail change, test result, and any borderline cases you handled or punted on.

Report length: under 350 words.

```
16	MEDIA_EXPLAIN_PROMPT = """Explain this image for durable future recall by a relationship-mediation assistant.
17	
18	Write a compact, queryable note. Include:
19	- visible people, setting, objects, screenshots/text, actions, and emotional tone when apparent
20	- relationship-relevant context that may matter later
21	- uncertainty where the image is ambiguous
22	
23	Do not identify unknown people or infer protected traits. Do not add advice. Return plain text only."""

26	def _extract_output_text(data: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
27	    output_text = data.get("output_text")
28	    if isinstance(output_text, str):
29	        return output_text.strip()
30	    pieces: list[str] = []
31	    for item in data.get("output", []) or []:
32	        for content in item.get("content", []) or []:
33	            text = content.get("text")
34	            if isinstance(text, str):
35	                pieces.append(text)
36	    return "\n".join(piece.strip() for piece in pieces if piece.strip()).strip()
37	
38	
39	def _analysis_payload(result: str | dict[str, Any], *, media_type: str = "image") -> dict[str, Any]:
40	    if isinstance(result, dict):
41	        payload = dict(result)
42	        explanation = payload.get("explanation") or payload.get("description") or payload.get("summary")
43	    else:
44	        payload = {}
45	        explanation = result
46	    explanation_text = str(explanation or "").strip()
47	    settings = get_settings()
48	    payload.update(
49	        {
50	            "kind": media_type,
51	            "provider": payload.get("provider") or "openai",
52	            "model": payload.get("model") or settings.vision_model,
53	            "detail": payload.get("detail") or settings.vision_detail,
54	            "explanation": explanation_text,
55	            # Backwards-compatible key for older code/tests and existing memories.
56	            "description": payload.get("description") or explanation_text,
57	        }
58	    )
59	    return payload
60	
61	
62	async def _openai_analyze(image_bytes: bytes, content_type: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
63	    settings = get_settings()
64	    data_url = f"data:{content_type};base64,{base64.b64encode(image_bytes).decode()}"
65	    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=settings.media_fetch_timeout_s) as client:
66	        response = await client.post(
67	            "https://api.openai.com/v1/responses",
68	            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.openai_api_key.get_secret_value()}"},
69	            json={
70	                "model": settings.vision_model,
71	                "input": [
72	                    {
73	                        "role": "user",
74	                        "content": [
75	                            {"type": "input_text", "text": MEDIA_EXPLAIN_PROMPT},
76	                            {"type": "input_image", "image_url": data_url, "detail": settings.vision_detail},
77	                        ],
78	                    }
79	                ],
80	            },
81	        )
82	    response.raise_for_status()
83	    explanation = _extract_output_text(response.json())
84	    if not explanation:
85	        raise ValueError("empty vision explanation")
86	    return _analysis_payload(explanation)
87	
88	
89	async def explain_stored_image(pool: Any, message_id) -> dict[str, Any]:
90	    row = await pool.fetchrow(
91	        """
92	        SELECT id, media_type, media_url
93	        FROM messages
94	        WHERE id=$1 AND deleted_at IS NULL
95	        """,
96	        message_id,
97	    )
98	    if row is None:
99	        raise ValueError("media message not found")
100	    if row["media_type"] != "image" or not row["media_url"]:
101	        raise ValueError("message is not an image with stored media")
102	    image_bytes, content_type = await storage.download_media(row["media_url"])
103	    analysis = _analysis_payload(await _openai_analyze(image_bytes, content_type))
104	    await pool.execute("UPDATE messages SET media_analysis=$1 WHERE id=$2", analysis, message_id)
105	    await record_llm_cost(pool, "vision", 0.001)
106	    return analysis
107	
108	
109	async def handle_image(
110	    pool: Any,
111	    message_id,
112	    media_id: str,
113	    user: User,
114	    coalescer: Any | None = None,
115	) -> None:
116	    paused = await system_state.is_paused(pool)
117	    should_enqueue = coalescer is not None and not paused
118	    image_bytes, content_type = await whatsapp.fetch_media(media_id)
119	    media_url = await storage.upload_media(
120	        get_settings().supabase_storage_bucket,
121	        f"image/{message_id}",
122	        image_bytes,
123	        content_type,
124	    )
125	    await pool.execute("UPDATE messages SET media_type='image', media_url=$1 WHERE id=$2", media_url, message_id)
126	
127	    if not await is_under_cap(pool, "vision"):
128	        await pool.execute(
129	            "UPDATE messages SET media_analysis=$1, processing_state='expired' WHERE id=$2",
130	            {"unavailable": "daily_cap"},
131	            message_id,
132	        )
133	        if not paused:
134	            await send_outbound(
135	                pool,
136	                user,
137	                "I can't analyze images right now -- could you describe it in text?",
138	                template_fallback=TemplateCall("media_failure", [user.name, "image"]),
139	            )
140	        return
141	
142	    try:
143	        analysis = _analysis_payload(await _openai_analyze(image_bytes, content_type))
144	    except Exception:
145	        await pool.execute(
146	            "UPDATE messages SET media_analysis=$1, processing_state='expired' WHERE id=$2",
147	            {"error": "vision_failed"},
148	            message_id,
149	        )
150	        if not paused:
151	            await send_outbound(
152	                pool,
153	                user,
154	                "I couldn't process your last image -- could you try resending or describe it in text?",
155	                template_fallback=TemplateCall("media_failure", [user.name, "image"]),
156	            )
157	    else:
158	        await pool.execute("UPDATE messages SET media_analysis=$1 WHERE id=$2", analysis, message_id)
159	        await record_llm_cost(pool, "vision", 0.001)
160	        if should_enqueue:
161	            await coalescer.add(user.id, message_id, user, source="media")
162
```

```
276	def twilio_form_to_meta_payload(form: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
277	    """Convert Twilio's application/x-www-form-urlencoded webhook to our Meta-shaped ingester."""
278	    from_value = form.get("From", "")
279	    phone = from_value.removeprefix("whatsapp:")
280	    wa_id = form.get("MessageSid") or form.get("SmsMessageSid") or str(uuid5(NAMESPACE_URL, repr(sorted(form.items()))))
281	    body = form.get("Body", "")
282	    num_media = int(form.get("NumMedia") or "0")
283	    profile_name = form.get("ProfileName") or phone
284	
285	    if num_media > 0:
286	        content_type = form.get("MediaContentType0", "")
287	        media_url = form.get("MediaUrl0", "")
288	        if content_type.startswith("image/"):
289	            message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "image", "image": {"id": media_url}}
290	        elif content_type.startswith("audio/"):
291	            message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "audio", "audio": {"id": media_url}}
292	        else:
293	            message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "document"}
294	    else:
295	        message = {"from": phone, "id": wa_id, "timestamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())), "type": "text", "text": {"body": body}}
296	
297	    return {
298	        "entry": [
299	            {
300	                "changes": [
301	                    {
302	                        "value": {
303	                            "contacts": [{"wa_id": phone, "profile": {"name": profile_name}}],
304	                            "messages": [message],
305	                        }
306	                    }
307	                ]
308	            }
309	        ]
310	    }
311
```

```
204	def _has_image_attachment(message: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
205	    return any(_is_image_attachment(att) for att in (message.get("attachments") or []))
206	
207	
208	def _is_image_attachment(attachment: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
209	    content_type = (attachment.get("content_type") or "").lower()
210	    if content_type.startswith("image/"):
211	        return True
212	    filename = (attachment.get("filename") or "").lower()
213	    return filename.endswith((".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp"))

216	def message_to_meta_payload(message: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
217	    author = message["author"]
218	    user_id = str(author["id"])
219	    name = _configured_partner_name(user_id) or author.get("global_name") or author.get("username") or user_id
220	    sent_at = datetime.now(UTC)
221	    if message.get("timestamp"):
222	        sent_at = datetime.fromisoformat(message["timestamp"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
223	    timestamp_str = str(int(sent_at.timestamp()))
224	    message_id = str(message["id"])
225	
226	    messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
227	    content = message.get("content") or ""
228	    if content:
229	        messages.append(
230	            {
231	                "from": user_id,
232	                "id": message_id,
233	                "timestamp": timestamp_str,
234	                "type": "text",
235	                "text": {"body": content},
236	            }
237	        )
238	
239	    for attachment in message.get("attachments") or []:
240	        if not _is_image_attachment(attachment):
241	            continue
242	        url = attachment.get("url") or attachment.get("proxy_url")
243	        if not url:
244	            continue
245	        attachment_id = str(attachment.get("id") or url)
246	        messages.append(
247	            {
248	                "from": user_id,
249	                "id": f"{message_id}:{attachment_id}",
250	                "timestamp": timestamp_str,
251	                "type": "image",
252	                "image": {"id": url},
253	            }
254	        )
255	
256	    if not messages:
257	        messages.append(
258	            {
259	                "from": user_id,
260	                "id": message_id,
261	                "timestamp": timestamp_str,
262	                "type": "text",
263	                "text": {"body": ""},
264	            }
265	        )
266	
267	    return {
268	        "entry": [
269	            {
270	                "changes": [
271	                    {
272	                        "value": {
273	                            "contacts": [
274	                                {
275	                                    "wa_id": user_id,
276	                                    "profile": {"name": name},
277	                                }

288	class DiscordGatewayBot:
289	    """Small Discord Gateway client for DM text ingestion."""
290	
291	    def __init__(self, pool: Any, coalescer: Any | None) -> None:
292	        self.pool = pool
293	        self.coalescer = coalescer
294	        self._closed = asyncio.Event()
295	        self._heartbeat_task: asyncio.Task | None = None
296	        self._gateway_loop = DiscordGatewayLoop(
297	            GatewayCallbacks(
298	                on_message_create=self._handle_message,
299	                on_message_update=self._handle_message_update,
300	                on_message_delete=self._handle_message_delete,
301	                on_reaction_add=self._handle_reaction_add,
302	                on_event=self._handle_gateway_event,
303	            )
304	        )
305	
306	    async def close(self) -> None:
307	        self._closed.set()
308	        if self._heartbeat_task is not None:
309	            self._heartbeat_task.cancel()
310	            with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
311	                await self._heartbeat_task
312	
313	    async def run_forever(self) -> None:
314	        while not self._closed.is_set():
315	            try:
316	                await self._run_once()
317	            except asyncio.CancelledError:
318	                raise
319	            except Exception:
320	                logger.exception("discord gateway loop failed")
321	                await asyncio.sleep(5)
322	
323	    async def _run_once(self) -> None:
324	        async with websockets.connect("wss://gateway.discord.gg/?v=10&encoding=json") as ws:
325	            hello = json.loads(await ws.recv())
326	            interval = hello["d"]["heartbeat_interval"] / 1000
327	            self._heartbeat_task = asyncio.create_task(self._heartbeat(ws, interval))
328	            await ws.send(
329	                json.dumps(
330	                    {
331	                        "op": 2,
332	                        "d": {
333	                            "token": _token(),
334	                            "intents": (1 << 12) | (1 << 14) | (1 << 15),
335	                            "properties": {"os": "macos", "browser": "veas", "device": "veas"},
336	                        },
337	                    }
338	                )
339	            )
340	            async for raw in ws:
341	                event = json.loads(raw)
342	                if event.get("op") == 0:
343	                    await self._gateway_loop.dispatch_payload(event)
344	                if self._closed.is_set():
345	                    break
346	
347	    async def _heartbeat(self, ws: Any, interval: float) -> None:
348	        with contextlib.suppress(websockets.exceptions.ConnectionClosed):
349	            while not self._closed.is_set():
350	                await asyncio.sleep(interval)
351	                await ws.send(json.dumps({"op": 1, "d": None}))
352	
353	    async def _handle_gateway_event(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
354	        if payload.get("t") != "TYPING_START" or self.coalescer is None:
355	            return
356	        pacer = getattr(self.coalescer, "pacer", None)
357	        if pacer is None:
358	            return
359	        data = payload.get("d")
360	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
361	            return
362	        user_id = str(data.get("user_id", ""))
363	        if not user_id or not is_allowed_discord_user(user_id):
364	            return
365	        typing_user = await upsert_user(
366	            self.pool,
367	            _configured_partner_name(user_id) or user_id,
368	            _discord_user_id(user_id),
369	            get_settings().default_user_timezone,
370	        )
371	        pacer.mark_user_typing(typing_user.id, channel_id=str(data.get("channel_id") or ""))
372	
373	    async def _handle_message(self, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
374	        from app.services.inbound import process_inbound
375	
376	        if message.get("author", {}).get("bot"):
377	            return
378	        author_id = str(message.get("author", {}).get("id", ""))
379	        if not is_allowed_discord_user(author_id):
380	            logger.warning("dropping non-whitelisted discord user %s", author_id)
381	            return
382	        if not message.get("content") and not _has_image_attachment(message):
383	            return
384	        await process_inbound(self.pool, message_to_meta_payload(message), self.coalescer)
385	
386	    async def _handle_message_update(self, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
387	        author_id = str(message.get("author", {}).get("id", ""))
388	        if not author_id or not is_allowed_discord_user(author_id):
389	            return
390	        if "content" not in message:
391	            return
392	        await self.pool.execute(
393	            """
394	            UPDATE messages
395	            SET edit_history = COALESCE(edit_history, '[]'::jsonb)
396	                    || jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('content', content, 'at', now())),
397	                content = $1,
398	                content_encrypted = $2,
399	                edited_at = now()
```

```
1	"""Per-user inbound burst coalescing."""
2	
3	import asyncio
4	from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
5	from dataclasses import dataclass
6	from typing import Any
7	from uuid import UUID
8	
9	from resident_chat_runtime.coalescing import AsyncBurstCoalescer, BurstBatch
10	
11	from app.models.user import User
12	from app.services.pacer import PacingDecision
13	
14	
15	@dataclass
16	class _Burst:
17	    message_ids: list[UUID]
18	    user: User
19	    first_seen_at: float
20	    source: str = "live"
21	
22	
23	class BurstCoalescer:
24	    def __init__(
25	        self,
26	        on_burst_complete: Callable[[list[UUID], User], Awaitable[None]],
27	        *,
28	        debounce_seconds: float = 10.0,
29	        max_seconds: float = 30.0,
30	        pacer: Any | None = None,
31	        on_paced_answer: Callable[[list[UUID], User, PacingDecision], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
32	        on_paced_reaction: Callable[[list[UUID], User, PacingDecision], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
33	        on_live_typing: Callable[[User, asyncio.Event], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
34	    ) -> None:
35	        self.on_burst_complete = on_burst_complete
36	        self.on_paced_answer = on_paced_answer
37	        self.on_paced_reaction = on_paced_reaction
38	        self.on_live_typing = on_live_typing
39	        self.pacer = pacer
40	        self.debounce_seconds = debounce_seconds
41	        self.max_seconds = max_seconds
42	        self._bursts: dict[UUID, _Burst] = {}
43	        self._locks: dict[UUID, asyncio.Lock] = {}
44	        self._wait_tasks: dict[UUID, asyncio.Task] = {}
45	        self._live_typing_tasks: dict[UUID, asyncio.Task[None]] = {}
46	        self._live_typing_stops: dict[UUID, asyncio.Event] = {}
47	        self._coalescer: AsyncBurstCoalescer[UUID, UUID] = AsyncBurstCoalescer(
48	            self._fire_batch,
49	            idle_delay=debounce_seconds,
50	            max_delay=max_seconds,
51	        )
52	
53	    async def add(self, user_id: UUID, message_id: UUID, user: User, *, source: str = "live") -> None:
54	        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
55	        lock = self._locks.setdefault(user_id, asyncio.Lock())
56	        async with lock:
57	            wait_task = self._wait_tasks.pop(user_id, None)
58	            if wait_task is not None:
59	                wait_task.cancel()
60	            burst = self._bursts.get(user_id)
61	            if burst is None:
62	                burst = _Burst(message_ids=[], user=user, first_seen_at=loop.time(), source=source)
63	                self._bursts[user_id] = burst
64	            burst.message_ids.append(message_id)
65	            burst.user = user
66	            burst.source = self._merge_source(burst.source, source)
67	            if source == "live" and self.on_live_typing is not None and user_id not in self._live_typing_tasks:
68	                stop_event = asyncio.Event()
69	                self._live_typing_stops[user_id] = stop_event
70	                self._live_typing_tasks[user_id] = asyncio.create_task(self.on_live_typing(user, stop_event))
71	            await self._coalescer.submit(user_id, message_id)
72	
73	    async def add_burst(self, user_id: UUID, message_ids: list[UUID], user: User) -> None:
74	        await self.on_burst_complete(message_ids, user)
75	
76	    async def _fire(self, user_id: UUID) -> None:
77	        await self._coalescer.flush(user_id)
78	
79	    async def _fire_batch(self, batch: BurstBatch[UUID, UUID]) -> None:
80	        lock = self._locks.setdefault(batch.key, asyncio.Lock())
81	        async with lock:
82	            burst = self._bursts.pop(batch.key, None)
83	            if burst is None:
84	                return
85	            await self._handle_ready_burst(batch.key, burst)
86	
87	    def snapshot(self) -> dict[UUID, Any]:
88	        return self._bursts
89	
90	    async def _handle_ready_burst(self, user_id: UUID, burst: _Burst) -> None:
91	        message_ids = list(burst.message_ids)
92	        await self._stop_live_typing(user_id)
93	        if self.pacer is None:
94	            # Runtime contract: on_burst_complete(triggering_message_ids: list[UUID], user).
95	            await self.on_burst_complete(message_ids, burst.user)
96	            return
97	
98	        decision = await self.pacer.decide_and_record(burst.user, message_ids, source=burst.source)
99	        if decision.action == "wait":
100	            self._bursts[user_id] = burst
101	            self._wait_tasks[user_id] = asyncio.create_task(self._fire_after_wait(user_id, max(0.0, decision.wait_s)))
102	            return
103	        if decision.action == "answer":
104	            await self._call_paced_answer(message_ids, burst.user, decision)
105	            return
106	        if decision.action == "react":
107	            if self.on_paced_reaction is not None:
108	                await self.on_paced_reaction(message_ids, burst.user, decision)
109	            await self._mark_processed(message_ids)
110	            return
111	        if decision.action == "silence":
112	            await self._mark_processed(message_ids)
113	            return
114	        await self._call_paced_answer(message_ids, burst.user, decision)
115	
116	    async def _stop_live_typing(self, user_id: UUID) -> None:
117	        stop_event = self._live_typing_stops.pop(user_id, None)
118	        task = self._live_typing_tasks.pop(user_id, None)
119	        if stop_event is not None:
120	            stop_event.set()
121	        if task is not None:
122	            try:
123	                await task
124	            except Exception:
125	                pass
126	
127	    async def _fire_after_wait(self, user_id: UUID, wait_s: float) -> None:
128	        try:
129	            await asyncio.sleep(wait_s)
130	            lock = self._locks.setdefault(user_id, asyncio.Lock())
131	            async with lock:
132	                self._wait_tasks.pop(user_id, None)
133	                burst = self._bursts.pop(user_id, None)
134	                if burst is not None:
135	                    await self._handle_ready_burst(user_id, burst)
136	        except asyncio.CancelledError:
137	            raise
138	
139	    async def _call_paced_answer(self, message_ids: list[UUID], user: User, decision: PacingDecision) -> None:
140	        if self.on_paced_answer is not None:
141	            await self.on_paced_answer(message_ids, user, decision)
142	            return
143	        await self.on_burst_complete(message_ids, user)
144	
145	    async def _mark_processed(self, message_ids: list[UUID]) -> None:
146	        pool = getattr(self.pacer, "pool", None)
147	        if pool is None or not message_ids:
148	            return
149	        await pool.execute(
150	            "UPDATE messages SET processing_state='processed' WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[]) AND processing_state='raw'",
151	            message_ids,
152	        )
153	
154	    def _merge_source(self, existing: str, incoming: str) -> str:
155	        if existing == incoming:
156	            return existing
157	        priority = {
158	            "recovery": 4,
159	            "catch_up": 4,
160	            "media": 3,
161	            "mixed": 2,
162	            "live": 1,
163	        }
164	        existing_priority = priority.get(existing, 2)
165	        incoming_priority = priority.get(incoming, 2)
166	        if existing_priority > incoming_priority:
167	            return existing
168	        if incoming_priority > existing_priority:
169	            return incoming
170	        if existing in {"recovery", "catch_up"}:
171	            return existing
172	        if incoming in {"recovery", "catch_up"}:
173	            return incoming
174	        return "mixed"
175
```

> AGENT

Now I have a good understanding. Let me design the fix.

**Choice: Option A** - in `process_inbound`, detect when the value contains both text and image messages from the same source, and process images BEFORE the text item enqueues to coalescer. The cleanest approach: collect all images first, await `handle_image` for each (which writes `media_analysis` synchronously, and on success enqueues `source="media"`), then when processing the text item, also enqueue under `source="media"` so it merges with the image's burst. But the simpler approach: when both text and image are in the same value, process images first; the image's `coalescer.add(..., source="media")` (after vision completes) will populate the burst. The text add can happen after — but then the issue is the text add happens BEFORE the image finishes (since they iterate sequentially). Actually wait — looking again, `process_inbound` iterates messages sequentially in a single async function. If images come AFTER text items in the iteration order, then text is added first, possibly debounced/fired. But `message_to_meta_payload` puts text first then images.

Let me reorder: process images BEFORE text within a single value. That ensures `handle_image` (which awaits OpenAI) completes before the text-driven add. By the time text is added to the coalescer, `media_analysis` is already populated. Both adds will go into the same burst (debounced 10s).

But wait — does `handle_image` actually block on OpenAI? Yes, it awaits `_openai_analyze` synchronously. So text won't fire until vision completes. 

The async pool concern: `handle_image` does pool operations (UPDATE), then awaits OpenAI (no pool held), then UPDATE again. It does not hold a pool slot during OpenAI. Good.

Let me implement this. I'll process all images first within each `value`, then process the rest in original order.

> AGENT

Wait — there's still a subtle problem. After image is processed, `handle_image` calls `coalescer.add(..., source="media")`. That starts the burst timer (debounce 10s). Then the text item is processed and calls `coalescer.add(..., source="live")` which extends the burst (cancels wait, adds, restarts debounce). Both message_ids are in the same burst. That's fine.

But what about the case where vision fails or daily-cap is exceeded? In daily-cap path, `handle_image` returns without calling `coalescer.add`. In failure path, same — no coalescer add. Then the text item is processed normally and fires its own turn. media_analysis still populated (with `{"error":"vision_failed"}` or `{"unavailable":"daily_cap"}`). Good — the text turn will see the image's media_analysis through hot context.

Pure-image (no text): only image item. handle_image runs, calls coalescer.add(media). Same as before.

Pure-text: no image. No reordering effect.

Multiple images: all processed sequentially first. Each one awaits its own vision call, then the text fires. Good.

```
12	    if normalized.startswith("discord:"):
```

> AGENT

Done.

---

**Report**

**Files changed:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/app/services/vision.py` — rewrote `MEDIA_EXPLAIN_PROMPT`.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/app/services/inbound.py` — reordered messages within each inbound `value` so images run before text/other types.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/tests/test_media.py` — added two regression tests (success + vision-failure paths).

**Race-fix option chosen: A** — sorted the per-`value` message list so `wa_type == "image"` items run first; `vision.handle_image` blocks on OpenAI before the text item ever reaches `coalescer.add(..., source="live")`, so by the time the text-driven add lands the image's `media_analysis` is already populated. Platform-agnostic, fixes Discord and WhatsApp identically. No coalescer/pacer changes; no pool slot held during the OpenAI call (`handle_image` releases between UPDATEs).

**`vision_detail`:** already defaults to `"high"` in `app/config.py:44`. No change needed.

**New `MEDIA_EXPLAIN_PROMPT` verbatim:**
```
Describe this image for durable future recall by a relationship-mediation assistant.

Return plain text in two parts:

1. A compact description paragraph covering: visible people, setting, objects, actions, emotional tone when apparent, and relationship-relevant context that may matter later. Note uncertainty where the image is ambiguous.

2. A line that begins exactly with "Visible text:" followed by a verbatim transcription of every readable string in the image, in natural reading order. This includes UI labels, screenshot message bodies (preserve speaker/sender attributions and message order if discernible), captions, signs, watermarks, and handwriting if legible. Preserve casing, punctuation, line breaks, and emoji as they appear. Do not paraphrase or summarize the text. If a portion is partially obscured or unreadable, transcribe what you can and mark the unclear span with "[unclear]" rather than guessing. If there is no readable text in the image, write "Visible text: none".

Safety rails: do not identify unknown people, do not infer protected traits, do not add advice or interpretation beyond what is visibly evident. Plain text only -- no markdown headers or code fences.
```

**Test result:** `uv run pytest -q` → 300 passed, 3 skipped, 2 failed. The two failures are the pre-existing `test_hot_context.py::test_render_hot_context_respects_default_token_budget` / `::test_render_hot_context_truncates_without_dropping_oob` baseline noted in the brief. New count = baseline + 2 (the two I added).

**Edge cases handled:** vision failure (test asserts text reply still fires, `{"error":"vision_failed"}` visible), pure-text and pure-image messages unchanged, multiple images all awaited before text via the sort. **Punted:** daily-cap path isn't exercised by a new test (existing `test_image_spend_over_threshold_still_runs_vision` already covers `handle_image`); the reordering fix applies trivially since the cap path returns synchronously after writing `{"unavailable":"daily_cap"}`. WhatsApp inherits the fix automatically — no separate follow-up needed.
